## Semantic Chunks
### Section: Introduction
- **Level**: `intro`
- **Hash**: `9ea49de4b3fe0ec25f9743f928b4bf1356d8678a66b3fc8375d4feb937f0a72a`
- **Best Competitor Match**: `/learning/2026/8/can-a-plagiarism-checker-automatically-check-canvas-assignments` (Similarity: 0.9291)

```text
Yes. With Checkmark Plagiarism's LTI 1.3 integration, teachers can view complete document writing history and video playback replays directly inside Canvas SpeedGrader—verifying active typing hours, revision depth, thinking pauses, and clipboard paste events for every Canvas assignment submission.

Canvas LMS natively accepts file uploads and text submissions, but it does not capture the real-time writing process. If a student submits a Word document or pastes an essay into Canvas, the teacher has no native way to know whether the paper was written over four days with deep revisions or pasted from ChatGPT in two seconds. By integrating Checkmark's Writing History Engine into Canvas SpeedGrader , educators gain full visibility into the creation timeline of every submission.

Below is a detailed guide on how writing history works in Canvas and how educators use it to verify authentic student scholarship.

Checkmark Plagiarism powers Canvas process verification by pairing essay writing playback with AI detection , plagiarism detection , autograding , and native Canvas LMS integration .
```

### Section: The 4 Writing History Features Inside Canvas SpeedGrader
- **Level**: `h2`
- **Hash**: `cab75bf1676dff05790a29aa92fc7ee3dcc48918e26575be4624b35c6b957291`
- **Best Competitor Match**: `/learning/2026/8/can-teachers-view-ai-detection-results-in-canvas-speedgrader` (Similarity: 0.8625)

```text
1. 15-Second Video Playback Replay

An embedded video player inside SpeedGrader accelerates hours of student drafting into a smooth visual replay showing text creation and self-editing.

2. Active Drafting Time & Session Metrics

Displays the exact total hours of active keyboard engagement (e.g., 4.2 hours) and the number of distinct drafting sessions across calendar dates.

3. Backspace & Revision Depth Tracking

Quantifies character deletions and sentence restructuring: authentic human drafting exhibits a healthy 15% to 30% backspace rate.

4. Original Paste Payload Capture

Flags every external paste event, recording the exact timestamp, character count, and raw unedited text before any subsequent edits occurred.
```

### Section: Why Writing History Is Essential for Canvas Grading
- **Level**: `h2`
- **Hash**: `4e2781191050362074f1904ca739f89998cf2d2cfde27d569b59c3c2e1c90e89`
- **Best Competitor Match**: `/learning/2026/8/should-teachers-check-writing-history-before-running-an-ai-detector` (Similarity: 0.8815)

```text
Integrating writing history directly into Canvas SpeedGrader provides two decisive pedagogical safeguards:

Instant False Positive Exoneration: If an articulate student's paper triggers a high AI score due to formal vocabulary, the teacher clicks "Play" in SpeedGrader. Seeing 4 hours of typing and 25% backspaces immediately dismisses the alert in seconds. 
 Conclusive Misconduct Proof: If an essay was generated by AI or copied from a friend, the timeline shows 1,500 words appearing in a single 1-second paste event with zero active typing, providing unassailable evidence for student conferences. 
 Encourages Iterative Writing: Students who know their drafting process is visible are motivated to draft over multiple days rather than procrastinating or seeking shortcuts.

Read more in how Checkmark writing process analysis works .
```

### Section: Comparison: Standard Canvas Submissions vs. Checkmark Writing History in Canvas
- **Level**: `h2`
- **Hash**: `5aa9032314881edfef9555e6a0e8a64cf1203eff65221a7e6d8f63cf83c005d1`
- **Best Competitor Match**: `/learning/2026/8/how-do-i-check-an-assignment-for-plagiarism-in-canvas` (Similarity: 0.8556)

```text
Standard Canvas Submissions (Black Box)

Displays only the final static submitted file. 
 Zero insight into drafting time or session counts. 
 Cannot tell if text was typed or copy-pasted. 
 Leaves teachers guessing about student authorship.

Checkmark Writing History in Canvas (Transparent)

Embedded 15-second video replay in SpeedGrader. 
 Quantifies active typing hours, sessions, and edits. 
 Logs exact clipboard paste timestamps and payloads. 
 Provides objective physical proof of human creation.
```

### Section: A 5-Step Educator Protocol for Writing History in Canvas
- **Level**: `h2`
- **Hash**: `b6d65f9d41098177b7ef04e87deda1f91529c610af78d880ee4b89e86620cb7d`
- **Best Competitor Match**: `/learning/2026/8/can-writing-history-reveal-humanized-ai-content` (Similarity: 0.8605)

```text
Canvas Writing History Workflow:

1. Enable Checkmark Plagiarism & Playback on your Canvas assignment. 
 2. Open Canvas SpeedGrader after student submissions are turned in. 
 3. Review the Active Drafting Hours metric displayed in the assessment sidebar. 
 4. Click "Play" on the 15-second playback video to observe drafting evolution and revisions. 
 5. Verify that backspace rates exceed 15% and confirm human composition with confidence.
```
